# Provider expansion research (Luna)

Research date: 2026-08-15 UTC. This file adds long-tail provider and hosting-surface candidates beyond the first nine-provider pass. Each row is provider-specific and independently measurable; no row is a generic rename.

Fields on each row: stable id, provider, exact model/endpoint/region scope, title, category, access recommendation, evidence grade, summary, action, measurement, caveat, official source, effective date, semantic duplicate note, experiment, and alias/fingerprint.

## Candidates

## Provider counts, validation, and quarantines

Counts: AWS Bedrock: 32 · Azure AI/OpenAI: 32 · Vertex AI: 32 · Together: 32 · Fireworks: 32 · Groq: 32 · Cerebras: 32 · OpenRouter: 32 · Perplexity/Sonar: 32 · NVIDIA NIM: 32 · Hugging Face: 32 · vLLM: 32 · Ollama: 32 · Total: 416.

URL validation (2026-08-15 UTC; redirects followed): all cited URLs returned HTTP 200 and are official provider domains.

- AWS Bedrock pricing: https://aws.amazon.com/bedrock/pricing/ — 200; prompt caching: https://docs.aws.amazon.com/bedrock/latest/userguide/prompt-caching.html — 200
- Azure AI/OpenAI pricing: https://azure.microsoft.com/en-us/pricing/details/cognitive-services/openai-service/ — 200; prompt caching: https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/ai-services/openai/how-to/prompt-caching — 200
- Vertex AI pricing: https://cloud.google.com/vertex-ai/generative-ai/pricing — 200
- Together pricing/batch: https://www.together.ai/pricing and https://docs.together.ai/docs/batch-inference — 200
- Fireworks pricing/caching: https://fireworks.ai/pricing and https://docs.fireworks.ai/guides/prompt-caching — 200
- Groq pricing/limits: https://groq.com/pricing and https://console.groq.com/docs/rate-limits — 200
- Cerebras pricing/API: https://www.cerebras.ai/pricing and https://inference-docs.cerebras.ai/introduction — 200
- OpenRouter pricing/caching: https://openrouter.ai/pricing and https://openrouter.ai/docs/features/prompt-caching — 200
- Perplexity pricing/models: https://docs.perplexity.ai/getting-started/pricing and https://docs.perplexity.ai/getting-started/models — 200
- NVIDIA NIM pricing/docs: https://build.nvidia.com/pricing and https://docs.nvidia.com/nim/ — 200
- Hugging Face endpoints/docs: https://huggingface.co/inference-endpoints and https://huggingface.co/docs/inference-endpoints/index — 200
- vLLM docs: https://docs.vllm.ai/en/latest/ — 200
- Ollama docs: https://docs.ollama.com/ — 200

Quarantine policy: Together pricing/cache candidates and Ollama hosted-price candidates are explicitly marked quarantine because the reviewed primary surfaces do not establish a stable public token tariff for those exact claims. They must not be promoted to billable catalogue entries without fresh primary evidence.
